# config/debt-baseline.txt: the count of readability debt in each tracked source file (`python3 tools/psxd_floor.py --help` lists the kinds).
# `python3 tools/psxd_floor.py debt` fails when a count is over its row, and when a row is over its count.
# After a change, run `python3 tools/psxd_floor.py debt --update`. In `mode admit` a rise needs `--admit` and is printed.
# `python3 tools/psxd_floor.py debt --down` switches to `mode down`, one way: from then on a count may only fall.
# A `skip` line names files that the checker does not read.
mode admit
